------------------------------------------------------------
文件：app\products\[category]\[slug]\page.tsx
------------------------------------------------------------

     4: 
     5:    文件路径：
     6:    app/products/[category]/[slug]/page.tsx
     7: 
     8:    路由说明：
     9:    1. /products/{category}/{slug}
    10:    2. 如果 slug 命中 product-route-map.ts，则显示产品类型筛选页
    11:       示例：/products/pumps/plunger-pumps
    12:    3. 如果 category === "control"，则显示智控模块详情页
    13:       示例：/products/control/abd-air-bubble-detector
    14:    4. 如果没有命中产品类型路由，则继续按旧逻辑显示产品详情页
    15:    5. 这样可以保留原有产品详情页，同时支持新的产品中心 SEO 路径
    16:    6. 柱塞泵具体型号详情页已单独使用：
    17:       /products/pumps/plunger-pumps/[slug]
    18: ========================================================= */
    19: 
    20: import type { Metadata } from "next";
    21: import { notFound } from "next/navigation";
    22: import { Suspense } from "react";
    23: 
    24: import ProductPageSkeleton from "@/components/common/ProductPageSkeleton";

    63:   { category: "tubing", slug: "pfa-tubing" },
    64: ];
    65: 
    66: /* =========================================================
    67:    智控模块静态路由参数
    68: 
    69:    说明：
    70:    1. 当前项目使用 output: export；
    71:    2. dynamicParams = false 时，所有动态路径必须在 generateStaticParams 中列出；
    72:    3. 所以 ABD / PDM5 详情页必须显式加入静态参数。
    73: ========================================================= */
    74: function getControlModuleStaticParams() {
    75:   return getControlModuleDetailSlugs().map((slug) => ({
    76:     category: CONTROL_CATEGORY_ID,
    77:     slug,
    78:   }));
    79: }
    80: 
    81: /* =========================================================
    82:    智控模块详情数据适配器
    83: 

    83: 
    84:    说明：
    85:    1. control-module-detail.generated.ts 是智控模块自己的数据结构；
    86:    2. ProductDetailClient 使用的是统一详情页展示结构；
    87:    3. 这里把智控数据转成 ProductDetailClient 可以直接渲染的字段；
    88:    4. 不新建独立页面，不新建独立样式，继续复用公共详情页。
    89: ========================================================= */
    90: 
    91: const CONTROL_MODULE_DETAIL_IMAGE_MAP: Record<string, string> = {
    92:   "abd-air-bubble-detector": "/images/products/control/foreach-abd-air-bubble-detector.webp",
    93:   "pdm5-pressure-sensor": "/images/products/control/foreach-pdm5-pressure-sensor.webp",
    94: };
    95: 
    96: function getControlModuleProductDetailData(detail: ControlModuleDetail) {
    97:   const controlModuleMainImage =
    98:     CONTROL_MODULE_DETAIL_IMAGE_MAP[detail.slug] ||
    99:     (detail as any).mainImage ||
   100:     ((detail as any).images && (detail as any).images[0]) ||
   101:     (detail as any).image ||
   102:     "/images/logo/foreach-logo-color.svg";
   103: 

    84:    说明：
    85:    1. control-module-detail.generated.ts 是智控模块自己的数据结构；
    86:    2. ProductDetailClient 使用的是统一详情页展示结构；
    87:    3. 这里把智控数据转成 ProductDetailClient 可以直接渲染的字段；
    88:    4. 不新建独立页面，不新建独立样式，继续复用公共详情页。
    89: ========================================================= */

文件：components\products\detail\ProductDetailClient.tsx
------------------------------------------------------------

   547: 
   548:   针系列详情页复用公共 ProductDetailClient。
   549:   这里单独识别针系列数据，避免底部 CTA 回退到其他产品系列，或直接不显示。
   550: */
   551: function isProbeDetailData(data: any): boolean {
   552:   return (
   553:     data?.sourceType === "probe-detail" ||
   554:     data?.category === "probes" ||
   555:     data?.categoryLabel === "针系列" ||
   556:     data?.productTypeName === "采样针" ||
   557:     data?.productTypeName === "穿刺针" ||
   558:     data?.productTypeName === "清洗针" ||
   559:     data?.productTypeName === "搅拌桨"
   560:   );
   561: }
   562: 
   563: function getProbeDetailBottomCta(data: any) {
   564:   if (!isProbeDetailData(data)) {
   565:     return null;
   566:   }

   217: function inferKind(data: DetailRecord, pathname = ""): ProductKind {
   218:   const text = [pathname, data.detailHref, data.href, data.selectionHref, data.productTypeId, data.productTypeName, data.categoryLabel, data.slug, data.title, data.name].filter(Boolean).join(" ").toLowerCase();
   219:   const rules: Array<[ProductKind, RegExp]> = [
   220:     ["hard-tube-fitting", /hard[- ]tube|硬管/], ["bulkhead-barbed-fitting", /bulkhead[- ]barbed|穿板倒刺/], ["thread-to-barbed-fitting", /thread[- ]to[- ]barb|thread to barb|螺纹转倒刺/], ["quick-connect-fitting", /quick[- ]connect|快插|快速接头/], ["luer-fitting", /luer|鲁尔/], ["female-thread-adapter", /female[- ]thread|内螺纹/], ["barbed-fitting", /barbed[- ]fitting|barbed fitting|倒刺接头/], ["check-valve", /check[- ]valve|单向阀/], ["filter", /filter|过滤器/],
   221:     ["plunger-pump", /plunger[- ]pump|柱塞泵/], ["diaphragm-pump", /diaphragm[- ]pump|隔膜泵/], ["pipetting-pump", /pipett|移液泵/], ["syringe-pump", /syringe[- ]pump|注射泵/], ["valveless-pump", /valveless|无阀/], ["tubing", /tubing|管路|软管/],
   222:     ["high-pressure-valve", /high[- ]pressure[- ]valve|高压阀/], ["solenoid-valve", /solenoid[- ]valve|电磁阀/], ["rotary-valve", /rotary[- ]valve|旋转阀|转阀/], ["sampling-probe", /sampling[- ]probe|采样针/], ["piercing-probe", /piercing[- ]probe|穿刺针/], ["wash-probe", /wash[- ]probe|清洗针/], ["mixing-paddle", /mixing[- ]paddle|搅拌桨/], ["air-bubble-detector", /air[- ]bubble|气泡/], ["pressure-sensor", /pressure[- ]sensor|压力传感/],
   223:   ];
   224:   return rules.find(([, pattern]) => pattern.test(text))?.[0] || "product";
   225: }
   226: 
   227: function inferCategory(kind: ProductKind) {
   228:   if (kind.endsWith("pump")) return "pumps";
   229:   if (["barbed-fitting", "bulkhead-barbed-fitting", "hard-tube-fitting", "thread-to-barbed-fitting", "quick-connect-fitting", "luer-fitting", "female-thread-adapter", "filter", "check-valve"].includes(kind)) return "fittings";
   230:   if (kind === "tubing") return "tubing";
   231:   if (kind.endsWith("valve")) return "valves";
   232:   if (["sampling-probe", "piercing-probe", "wash-probe", "mixing-paddle"].includes(kind)) return "probes";
   233:   return "control";
